## المجموعة 44: المهاجم الرئيسي: البطاقة المرجعية

**المجلد:** `art/raw/v5/striker/`  
**المحادثة:** جديدة. لاعب رئيسي بكل الحركات وكل الاتجاهات. ابدأ بها: كل مجموعات هذه الشخصية ترفقها.

### 44.1 `ref.png`

البطاقة المرجعية: أمام، ثلاثة أرباع، جانب، خلف · بلا مرفق

```
A wide 3:2 landscape character reference card of a fictional male footballer in a plain pure white kit (white shirt, white shorts, white socks) and black boots, no logos or numbers (the game paints the team colours on the white), short dark brown hair, light olive skin, light stubble, athletic striker build.: four full body views side by side in one row at exactly the same scale, standing on the same ground line, relaxed with arms slightly away from the body. From left to right: front view facing the camera, three-quarter view facing right, side view facing right, back view. Photorealistic 3D render in the style of a modern console football game, lit by night stadium floodlights from high above and slightly behind: cool bluish white light with a bright rim light on the head, shoulders and arms, and slightly darker lower legs. Correct, natural anatomy in every pose: knees and elbows bend the natural way, both legs and both arms complete, feet pointing where the body moves, five fingers on each hand. The background is one flat, solid chroma-key green (#00FF00) with nothing else on it, no floor and no shadows, so each figure is easy to cut out. The image has no text, no numbers and no logos.
```

### 44.2 `faces.png`

الوجه: تركيز، فرحة، حسرة، غضب · **ارفق اللوحة الأولى**

```
Use the attached reference card for this exact same person. A wide 3:2 landscape image with four close-up portraits of him in a 2 by 2 grid, head and shoulders, three-quarter view facing right, each with a blurred night stadium and floodlights behind, separated by thin black lines. a fictional male footballer in a plain pure white kit (white shirt, white shorts, white socks) and black boots, no logos or numbers (the game paints the team colours on the white), short dark brown hair, light olive skin, light stubble, athletic striker build. Top left: focused and calm. Top right: screaming with joy. Bottom left: devastated, hands on head. Bottom right: angry, protesting. Photorealistic 3D render like a modern console football game, lit by cool stadium floodlights with a rim light on the hair. The image has no text, no numbers and no logos.
```

## المجموعة 45: المهاجم الرئيسي: الجري بالاتجاهات

**المجلد:** `art/raw/v5/striker/run/`  
**المحادثة:** جديدة. كل لوحة 3 إطارات متتالية (2 للحركات العريضة). ارفق البطاقة المرجعية، ومن الإطار الثاني فصاعداً ارفق معها اللوحة السابقة.

### 45.1 `run-side-01.png`

running: الإطارات 1 إلى 3 من 8 · **ارفق `art/raw/v5/striker/ref.png`**

```
Use the attached reference card for this exact same person: the same face, hair, body and clothes, at the same scale. A wide 3:2 landscape image with 3 animation frames side by side in one row: frames 1 to 3 of a 8-frame running sequence. The character is a fictional male footballer in a plain pure white kit (white shirt, white shorts, white socks) and black boots, no logos or numbers (the game paints the team colours on the white), short dark brown hair, light olive skin, light stubble, athletic striker build. He is seen from the side, facing and moving to the right. These are consecutive animation frames for a 2D game sprite sheet, evenly spaced in time, so each frame moves on only slightly from the one before. Every frame shows the whole figure from head to feet at exactly the same scale, standing on the same invisible ground line (the ground line is at the same height in every frame), with clear space between frames and nothing cut off. Left frame (frame 1): Running at speed: the right foot just touching down in front of the body heel first, the left leg stretched behind, the left arm forward. Middle frame (frame 2): Running at speed: the right foot flat under the hips taking the weight, knee slightly bent, the left leg swinging forward low. Right frame (frame 3): Running at speed: the right leg pushing off behind, the left knee passing forward at hip height. Photorealistic 3D render in the style of a modern console football game, lit by night stadium floodlights from high above and slightly behind: cool bluish white light with a bright rim light on the head, shoulders and arms, and slightly darker lower legs. Correct, natural anatomy in every pose: knees and elbows bend the natural way, both legs and both arms complete, feet pointing where the body moves, five fingers on each hand. The background is one flat, solid chroma-key green (#00FF00) with nothing else on it, no floor and no shadows, so each figure is easy to cut out. The image has no text, no numbers and no logos.
```
